- **montaj-fon**: Background department of the montage studio. Use when, based on the meaning breakdown, the background needs to be replaced or you need to decide whether it is worth replacing: green background — chroma key with despill, ordinary wall — AI matting with a halo check, otherwise leave it as is. Takes the background from the owner's library (the "Фоны" folder, catalog config/fony-vladelca.md) — the one that best fits the meaning of the speech — and matches the lighting. Returns a file with the new background or a reasoned refusal. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-fon.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-grafika**: Отдел графики монтажной студии. Использовать, когда есть смысловой разбор, чистовик речи и вставки: собирает spec.json ролика в выбранном стиле — слои поверх непрерывного кадра, окна говорящего, счётчики, сетки, списки, слово-эхо, финальный призыв. Раскладывает всё вне лица и вне полосы субтитров, привязывает появление к словам. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-grafika.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-grafika**: Graphics department of the montage studio. Use when you have the meaning breakdown, the clean speech cut and the inserts: assembles the reel's spec.json in the chosen style — layers over a continuous shot, speaker windows, counters, grids, lists, echo word, final call to action. Places everything off the face and off the caption strip, and ties each appearance to words. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-grafika.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-kachestvo**: Quality department of the montage studio. Use before montage on every source file: measures noise, skin-tone balance, face exposure, sharpness and sound, decides what to fix, and fixes only what went past the threshold. Separates fixing defects from the color look of a style. Returns kachestvo.json and the corrected files. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-kachestvo.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-kachestvo**: Отдел качества монтажной студии. Использовать до монтажа на каждом исходнике: меряет шум, баланс по тону кожи, экспозицию лица, резкость и звук, решает, что чинить, и чинит только то, что вышло за порог. Отделяет починку брака от цветового вида стиля. Отдаёт kachestvo.json и исправленные файлы. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-kachestvo.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-rezka**: Cutting department of the montage studio. Use after the meaning breakdown: cuts out pauses, failed takes, stretches of reading from notes and the off-screen prompter voice, and assembles a continuous clean speech cut with soft cuts. Decides by meaning where to cut and where to leave a pause for weight. Separates the prompter voice with the script `speechsplit.py`, cuts pauses with `jumpcut.py`. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-rezka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-rezka**: Отдел резки монтажной студии. Использовать после смыслового разбора: вырезает паузы, неудачные дубли, куски чтения с листа и закадровую подсказку, собирает непрерывный чистовик речи с мягкими стыками. Решает по смыслу, где резать, а где оставить паузу для веса. Подсказку отделяет скриптом `speechsplit.py`, паузы режет `jumpcut.py`. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-rezka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-sborka**: Отдел сборки монтажной студии. Использовать последним: проверяет спеку, собирает ролик, снимает лист ключевых кадров, прогоняет qc.py и отдаёт всё критикам кадра и удержания. Знает обходы, когда рендер заблокирован системой. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-sborka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-sborka**: Assembly department of the montage studio. Use last: checks the spec, assembles the video, takes a keyframe sheet, runs qc.py and hands the result to the montage director (there are no critics). Knows the workarounds when rendering is blocked by the system. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-sborka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-smysl**: Смысловой отдел монтажной студии. Использовать первым шагом монтажа любого ролика: расшифровывает речь, понимает тему, посыл, аудиторию и эмоцию, режет речь на смысловые биты и для каждого пишет, какая картинка нужна и какую искать нельзя, предлагает фон и стиль по каталогу. Отдаёт smysl.json, из которого работают все остальные отделы. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-smysl.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-smysl**: Meaning department of the montage studio. Use as the first step of montage for any video: transcribes the speech, understands the topic, message, audience and emotion, cuts the speech into meaning beats and writes for each one which picture is needed and which must not be searched for, proposes a background and a style from the catalog. - **montaj-titry**: Captions department of the montage studio. Use after graphics: writes Armenian captions by meaning — short, selling, not verbatim, but without distorting what was said; places them only where they are needed and removes them where the graphics already say the same. The Armenian is written by the department itself, following the factory's Armenian-language canon. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-titry.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-titry**: Отдел титров монтажной студии. Использовать после графики: пишет армянские субтитры по смыслу — короткие, продающие, не дословные, но без искажения сказанного; ставит их только там, где они нужны, и снимает там, где графика уже говорит то же. Армянский пишется сам, по канону армянского языка завода. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-titry.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-vstavki**: Inserts department of the montage studio. Use after the meaning breakdown: for every beat that needs a picture, it searches Pexels and Pixabay stock using the queries from the breakdown, picks by eye from a preview sheet, checks the clip over its full length, and converts it to the output format. Also works with the owner's own footage and a partner's video: crops, covers or paints over watermarks on material that is cleared for use. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-vstavki.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-vstavki**: Отдел вставок монтажной студии. Использовать после смыслового разбора: для каждого бита, где нужна картинка, ищет стоки на Pexels и Pixabay по запросам из разбора, выбирает глазами по листу превью, проверяет клип по всей длине, приводит к формату вывода. Работает и со своими кадрами владельца и видео партнёра: обрезает, перекрывает или закрашивает водяные знаки на материале с разрешением. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-vstavki.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-zvuk**: Sound department of the montage studio. Use after the picture is assembled: picks music along the emotional arc from the meaning breakdown, sets it 15–20 dB below the voice with ducking under speech, adds rare sound accents, mixes and masters loudness for the platform. - **moodforge-brand-architect**: Use when the moodforge-queen has a locked theme from Step 2 and needs Step 3 brand kit. - **moodforge-conventions**: REFERENCE DOCUMENT — DO NOT INVOKE AS AN AGENT. This file is the single source of truth for the protocols every moodforge worker must follow: the mandatory read protocol (state.json first, never re-read prior HTML bodies), the artifact frontmatter format (YAML header on every file produced), and the state_delta return contract (strict structured output to the queen). The moodforge-queen tells every worker to Read this file before doing anything else. Updating this file updates the rules for all 11 agents at once. ([PiyushMalik01/North](https://github.com/PiyushMalik01/North/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/moodforge/agents/moodforge-conventions.md) · 2 copies)

([PiyushMalik01/North](https://github.com/PiyushMalik01/North/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/moodforge/agents/moodforge-figma-pusher.md) · 2 copies)
- **moodforge-handoff-engineer**: Use when the moodforge-queen has approved brand kit + motion + screens (and optionally Figma) and needs Step 6 implementation handoff. ([PiyushMalik01/North](https://github.com/PiyushMalik01/North/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/moodforge/agents/moodforge-handoff-engineer.md) · 2 copies)
- **moodforge-inspiration-scout**: Use when the moodforge-queen has a new project with no inspiration brief yet. Never invoked directly — only by the queen. ([PiyushMalik01/North](https://github.com/PiyushMalik01/North/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/moodforge/agents/moodforge-inspiration-scout.md) · 2 copies)
- **moodforge-motion-director**: Use when the moodforge-queen has an approved brand kit from Step 3 and needs Step 3.5 motion review. - **moodforge-preview-server**: Internal helper agent. Use only when superpowers:brainstorming is unavailable and the moodforge-queen needs the zero-dependency localhost preview as a fallback. - **moodforge-queen**: Use when the user starts any visual design work — "design an app", "build a landing page", "make it look better", "redesign", "brand refresh", "mockup", "theme". The queen is the controller of the moodforge hive-mind. It reads project state, picks the current phase, dispatches the right worker agent, holds user-approval gates, and writes state.md. Never invoked by another agent — only by the user's top-level request. All other moodforge-* agents are workers spawned by this queen. ([PiyushMalik01/North](https://github.com/PiyushMalik01/North/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/moodforge/agents/moodforge-queen.md) · 2 copies)
- **moodforge-screen-designer**: Use when the moodforge-queen has approved brand kit + animations and needs Step 5 screens. - **moodforge-state-keeper**: Internal helper agent. Use only when the moodforge-queen needs to (a) repair a stale or mismatched sha256 between an artifact and its state.json record, (b) rebuild a corrupted state.json from filesystem evidence, (c) re-patch a missing or stale @moodforge frontmatter header on an artifact file, or (d) recover from a parallel-write race during the screen-designer fan-out. Owns hash verification and atomic disk writes. Never invoked by the user — only by the queen. ([PiyushMalik01/North](https://github.com/PiyushMalik01/North/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/moodforge/agents/moodforge-state-keeper.md) · 2 copies)

- **motion-mixer**: Sound reviewer that cannot listen and doesn't pretend to - reads Motion Lab audio-measure output against SOUND.md and writes numbered, measurement-quoting sound follow-ups (timing, levels over bed, loudness, band balance, limiter, seam, codec padding). - **motion-monitor**: Run a bounded read-only background monitor of a Reachy Mini behavior session — poll the Pollen daemon REST API at 10 Hz, tail `journalctl --user -u reachy-mini-daemon.service` and the running app's stdout in parallel, classify every detected anomaly per the four motion-anomaly-detection classes (A head-against-body self-collision, B jerky motion, C antenna wobble, D Stewart-limit knock), and write a JSON-Lines audit log under `~/.cache/reachy-mini-monitor/&lt;session-id&gt;.jsonl`. Returns a tight PASS/WARN/FAIL/ABORTED summary plus the audit-log path. Bounded 5–15 min session, never daemonized. Us… ([nolte/claude-reachy-mini](https://github.com/nolte/claude-reachy-mini/blob/HEAD/agents/motion-monitor.md))

- **motion-producer**: Producer for the motion-director skill. Checks how much quota is left and what the film will cost, and says so plainly when the film does not fit.
